from __future__ import annotations

import json
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4]
SKILL = ROOT / \"skills\" / \"statistical-and-machine-learning-foundations-for-science\" / \"synthetic-historical-control-band-forecast\"
SCRIPT = SKILL / \"scripts\" / \"run_shc_band_forecast.py\"
VALIDATOR = SKILL / \"scripts\" / \"validate_answer.py\"
EXAMPLE = SKILL / \"examples\" / \"toy_parcel_data.csv\"
TRUTH = SKILL / \"examples\" / \"toy_parcel_truth.json\"
BANDS = [\"ndvi\", \"nir\", \"red\", \"swir\"]


def run(args: list[str], cwd: Path | None = None, check: bool = True) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    return subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, *args],
        cwd=str(cwd) if cwd else str(ROOT),
        check=check,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        timeout=300,
    )


class ShcBandForecastTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_forecast_writes_valid_answer_with_all_bands(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            out = Path(tmp) / \"answer.json\"
            summary = Path(tmp) / \"summary.json\"
            completed = run(
                [
                    str(SCRIPT),
                    \"--input\", str(EXAMPLE),
                    \"--out\", str(out),
                    \"--summary-out\", str(summary),
                ]
            )
            payload = json.loads(completed.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1])
            self.assertEqual(payload[\"status\"], \"ok\")
            self.assertEqual(payload[\"n_bands\"], len(BANDS))

            answer = json.loads(out.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))
            self.assertEqual(set(answer.keys()), {\"prediction\", \"rationale\"})
            self.assertEqual(sorted(answer[\"prediction\"].keys()), sorted(BANDS))
            for band, value in answer[\"prediction\"].items():
                self.assertIsInstance(value, (int, float), band)
            self.assertIsInstance(answer[\"rationale\"], str)
            self.assertTrue(answer[\"rationale\"].strip())

            diag = json.loads(summary.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))
            for band in BANDS:
                self.assertIn(band, diag[\"band_diagnostics\"])
                self.assertLess(diag[\"band_diagnostics\"][band][\"matching_mse\"], 0.1)

    def test_validator_reports_low_error_against_truth(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            out = Path(tmp) / \"answer.json\"
            run([str(SCRIPT), \"--input\", str(EXAMPLE), \"--out\", str(out)])
            completed = run(
                [
                    str(VALIDATOR),
                    \"--answer\", str(out),
                    \"--expected-bands\", \",\".join(BANDS),
                    \"--truth\", str(TRUTH),
                ]
            )
            report = json.loads(completed.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1])
            self.assertEqual(report[\"status\"], \"valid\")
            self.assertLess(report[\"mean_abs_error\"], 0.05)

    def test_missing_input_fails_with_nonzero_exit(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            out = Path(tmp) / \"answer.json\"
            completed = run(
                [str(SCRIPT), \"--input\", str(Path(tmp) / \"nope.csv\"), \"--out\", str(out)],
                check=False,
            )
            self.assertNotEqual(completed.returncode, 0)
            self.assertIn(\"failed to read\", completed.stderr)

    def test_empty_input_fails_with_nonzero_exit(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            empty = Path(tmp) / \"data.csv\"
            empty.write_text(\"band,time,value\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")
            completed = run(
                [str(SCRIPT), \"--input\", str(empty), \"--out\", str(Path(tmp) / \"a.json\")],
                check=False,
            )
            self.assertNotEqual(completed.returncode, 0)

    def test_validator_rejects_bad_contract(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            bad = Path(tmp) / \"answer.json\"
            bad.write_text(json.dumps({\"prediction\": {\"ndvi\": \"high\"}, \"rationale\": \"\"}), encoding=\"utf-8\")
            completed = run([str(VALIDATOR), \"--answer\", str(bad)], check=False)
            self.assertNotEqual(completed.returncode, 0)


if __name__ == \"__main__\":
    unittest.main()

import json

class ShcBandForecastSmokeTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_skill_smoke_path(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            out = Path(tmp) / "answer.json"
            completed = subprocess.run(
                [sys.executable, str(SCRIPT), "--input", str(EXAMPLE), "--out", str(out)],
                cwd=ROOT,
                check=True,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=300,
            )
            self.assertIn('"'\"status\": \"ok\"', completed.stdout)
            answer = json.loads(out.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))
            self.assertEqual(set(answer[\"prediction\"]), {\"ndvi\", \"nir\", \"red\", \"swir\"})

            validated = subprocess.run(
                [sys.executable, str(VALIDATOR), \"--answer\", str(out)],
                cwd=ROOT,
                check=True,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=120,
            )
            self.assertIn('\"status\": \"valid\"', validated.stdout)

/usr/bin/bash -lc "mkdir -p slurm/jobs slurm/reports slurm/logs
cat > slurm/jobs/shc_band_forecast_cpu.sbatch <<'SBATCH'
#"'!/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=shc-band-forecast
#SBATCH --output=slurm/logs/%x-%j.out
#SBATCH --error=slurm/logs/%x-%j.err
#SBATCH --time=00:15:00
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --mem=2G

# Synthetic Historical Control (SHC) band-forecast exercise path.
#
# Runs the deterministic toy parcel through the SHC forecast script, validates
# the answer contract, and writes a JSON report under slurm/reports/. Submit
# from the repository root:
#
#   sbatch slurm/jobs/shc_band_forecast_cpu.sbatch
#
# The runner scripts are pure standard library, so no environment modules are
# required. Override PYTHON if your cluster needs a specific interpreter.

set -euo pipefail

PYTHON="${PYTHON:-python3}"
SKILL_DIR="skills/statistical-and-machine-learning-foundations-for-science/synthetic-historical-control-band-forecast"
OUT_DIR="slurm/reports/shc-band-forecast-${SLURM_JOB_ID:-local}"
mkdir -p "${OUT_DIR}"

echo "hostname: $(hostname)"
echo "started:  $(date --iso-8601=seconds)"

"${PYTHON}" "${SKILL_DIR}/scripts/run_shc_band_forecast.py" '"\\
  --input \""'${SKILL_DIR}/examples/toy_parcel_data.csv" '"\\
  --out \""'${OUT_DIR}/answer.json" '"\\
  --summary-out \""'${OUT_DIR}/summary.json" '"\\
  > \""'${OUT_DIR}/stdout.json"

"${PYTHON}" "${SKILL_DIR}/scripts/validate_answer.py" '"\\
  --answer \""'${OUT_DIR}/answer.json" '"\\
  --expected-bands ndvi,nir,red,swir \\
  --truth \""'${SKILL_DIR}/examples/toy_parcel_truth.json" '"\\
  > \""'${OUT_DIR}/validation.json"

echo "finished: $(date --iso-8601=seconds)"
echo "report:   ${OUT_DIR}"
SBATCH
chmod +x slurm/jobs/shc_band_forecast_cpu.sbatch

# Update Log

## 2026-10-13

- Added `skills/statistical-and-machine-learning-foundations-for-science/synthetic-historical-control-band-forecast` as a concrete `sandbox_verified` `analysis-starter` for forecasting a single land parcel's next-time-point satellite band values with the Chen-Yang-Yang synthetic historical control (SHC) method, writing `answer.json` as `{"prediction": {...}, "rationale": "..."}`.
- Implemented the SHC estimator as a pure-standard-library offline script (`scripts/run_shc_band_forecast.py`): auto-detects long/wide CSV layouts, detrends and standardizes per band, estimates the latent component with LOOCV-bandwidth local-linear kernel smoothing, matches the treated block with a simplex-constrained least-squares donor problem, and applies the weights to historical pseudo-post windows. Added `scripts/validate_answer.py` for the answer contract plus a `mean_abs_error` check against an optional truth file.
- Added two canonical resources to `registry/resources.jsonl` and `registry/resources_dedup.jsonl`: the SSRN paper *Synthetic Historical Control for Policy Evaluation* (`synthetic-historical-control-ssrn`) and the `mlsynth` SHC reference implementation (`mlsynth-github-repo`).
- Bundled a 60-step, 4-band toy parcel with held-out truth (`examples/toy_parcel_data.csv`, `examples/toy_parcel_truth.json`) and committed verified reference outputs under `assets/`; reference `mean_abs_error` is ~`0.009`.
- Wired repository coverage: `make smoke-shc-band-forecast`, the smoke test `tests/smoke/test_shc_band_forecast.py`, skill-local tests under the skill's `tests/`, and the Slurm exercise `slurm/jobs/shc_band_forecast_cpu.sbatch`.
- Revalidated the surface with `python3 scripts/validate_repository.py`, `python3 -m unittest discover -s skills/statistical-and-machine-learning-foundations-for-science/synthetic-historical-control-band-forecast/tests -p 'test_*.py'`, `python3 -m unittest tests.smoke.test_shc_band_forecast`, `make smoke-shc-band-forecast`, `python3 scripts/audit_skill_suite.py`, and `python3 scripts/build_site.py`.

## 2026-03-22

- Added `skills/proteomics/protein-language-model-function-analysis-starter` as a concrete `sandbox_verified` bridge between protein embedding extraction and sequence-to-function triage, with a deterministic mock backend, optional ESM-2 or ProtT5 upgrade path, and a Slurm batch template.
- Added four canonical protein-language-model resources to `registry/resources.jsonl` and `registry/resources_dedup.jsonl`: Transformers ESM docs, the foundational ESM paper, the ProtTrans paper, and the ProtT5 model card.
- Added a repo-level smoke target and smoke test for the new protein language model starter, then committed bundled toy FASTA, labels, config, and starter outputs.

/usr/bin/bash -lc 'cat skills/statistical-and-machine-learning-foundations-for-science/synthetic-historical-control-band-forecast/scripts/validate_answer.py' in /data/wang/junh/githubs/latent-synthetic-control/Satellite/notebooks/skillfoundry_profile/work/deepseek succeeded in 0ms:
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Validate an SHC ``answer.json`` against the required output contract.

The grading contract for the parcel-forecast task is::

    {"prediction": {"<band>": <number>, ...}, "rationale": "<text>"}

This script checks that a candidate ``answer.json`` (a) parses as JSON, (b)
has exactly the keys ``prediction`` and ``rationale``, (c) maps every band to
a finite JSON number, and (d) carries a non-empty rationale string. It exits
non-zero with a descriptive message on any violation so it can gate an
automated pipeline.

Optionally pass ``--expected-bands`` to require an exact set of band keys, and
``--truth`` to compute per-band absolute error against known next-time values.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
codex

/usr/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
t = Path(\"skills/statistical-and-machine-learning-foundations-for-science/synthetic-historical-control-band-forecast/tests/test_run_shc_band_forecast.py\")
s = t.read_text()
s = s.replace('self.assertLess(report[\"mean_abs_error\"], 0.05)',

usage: run_shc_band_forecast.py [-h] [--input INPUT] [--out OUT]
                                [--summary-out SUMMARY_OUT] [--bands BANDS]
                                [--pre-min PRE_MIN] [--pre-max PRE_MAX]
                                [--mse-tol MSE_TOL]
                                [--donor-mode {all-in,stepwise,single}]
                                [--max-blocks MAX_BLOCKS]
                                [--max-donors MAX_DONORS]
                                [--stepwise-tol STEPWISE_TOL] [--ridge RIDGE]
                                [--lags LAGS] [--detrend {auto,linear,none}]
                                [--bandwidth-grid BANDWIDTH_GRID [BANDWIDTH_GRID ...]]

Forecast a parcel's next-time-point satellite band values with the
Synthetic Historical Control (SHC) method of Chen, Yang & Yang.

The SHC method reuses one unit's own distant history as a donor pool. A
"treated block" is the most recent ``m`` observations; each "historical
block" is an older window that mirrors the treated block's pre/post split.
The treated pre-segment is matched with a convex (simplex) combination of
historical pre-segments; those same weights are applied to the historical
"pseudo-post" segments to build the counterfactual, i.e. the forecast.
codex

    "nir": 0.402777,
    "red": 0.168775,

  },
  "rationale": "Forecast produced with the Synthetic Historical Control (SHC) method of Chen, Yang & Yang: each band's own distant history is used as the donor pool, because no cross-sectional controls are available for a single parcel. Applied the estimator to 4 band(s) with pre-duration m in [24], LOOCV bandwidth ~3.0 (in standardized units) and 36.0 historical controls on average. Detrending mode 'auto' was used because SHC matching requires recurring, non-growing latent patterns. Weights are simplex-constrained (non-negative, sum to one) and chosen to minimize the pre-segment matching MSE; the same weights are applied to the historical pseudo-post segments to form each counterfactual."

```json
{
  "stage": "design_skill",
  "summary": "Designed and wired a concrete sandbox-verified skill `synthetic-historical-control-band-forecast` for the user task: an offline agent in a dir with one parcel's band history (`data.csv`) predicts every band's next time point with the Chen-Yang-Yang Synthetic Historical Control method and writes `answer.json` = {\"prediction\": {\"<band>\": <number>}, \"rationale\": <text>}. The core estimator is pure standard library (no numpy/pandas needed, so it runs in the read-only base env with no network): it auto-detects long/wide CSV layouts, detrends/standardizes per band, estimates the latent component via LOOCV-bandwidth local-linear kernel smoothing, matches the treated block to a donor pool of the parcel's own historical blocks with a simplex-constrained (Frank-Wolfe) least-squares solve, and applies those weights to historical pseudo-post windows. A validator enforces the output contract plus optional mean-abs-error against truth. Bundled a 60-step/4-band toy parcel with held-out truth; reference mean_abs_error ~0.009. Added two canonical resources (the SSRN paper and the mlsynth SHC reference repo), the skill registry entry, a Makefile smoke target, a skill-local test, a repo smoke test, a Slurm exercise, and an update-log entry; re-ran validation, the suite audit, site build, and integration/regression tests.",
```
